Path-ordered native generation characterization

P10d2b2b1 · 2026-10-11T20:05:32.306Z · v22.12.0 · linux/x64 · AMD Ryzen 7 5800H with Radeon Graphics. 3 alternating fresh processes per implementation/profile.

This source revision is superseded. Complete original graphs/facts and snapshots matched, and sampled memory decreased, but the compiled mixed cold/history latency guards failed at every size (cold +21.7/+17.4/+15.1%; history +13.8/+14.5/+11.8%). Medium warm time also missed its guard (+10.8%). The final implementation groups native dispatch by grammar family to avoid repeated loads while retaining six-grammar/16 MiB generation limits. This report preserves every completed observation and does not qualify the final revision.

Complete original facts equal across every run; local latency/memory guards: PASS
Shared sampled tree peak: 616.6 / 307.4 MiB (-50.1%). Total shared parsing: 2160 / 1615 ms (-25.2%). Isolated profiles: 8834 / 3795 ms (-57.0%). Both time guards permit at most ten percent regression; shared sampled memory must decrease.

Both implementations split native extraction from qualified literal-script compiler jobs and reap every native generation before loading the optional TypeScript compiler. The current indexed parser reserves at most six primary/auxiliary grammars and 16 MiB cumulative original UTF-8 input per generation. It awaits process exit before crossing a threshold or recovering a failed job. A pending-retirement guard blocks later native dispatch when retirement itself fails; conformance injects that failure repeatedly and proves recovery only after exit. A single allowed input over the byte threshold still parses, then retires before the next original. These are lifetime limits, not a WASM byte allocation or RSS cap. The V8 256 MiB old-space limit and ten-second per-job deadline are unchanged.

Twenty authored original grammar profiles run twice. Shared runs rotate only when a grammar or byte reservation would cross a limit; isolated runs start fresh native/compiler services per profile. Every complete fact digest, grammar/query hash, declaration, issue and truncation outcome agrees with the archived baseline and across repetitions. Before a new native generation and every compiler dispatch, the harness checks that the old native address spaces exited. The checked plain JavaScript native worker bundles only approved Codiluce helpers and starts without a TypeScript source loader; build and conformance checks require exact source equality and lazy job-local compiler imports.

A separate process polls all thread descendants every 25 ms with acknowledged start markers. RSS sums include parser children, can double count shared pages and miss short peaks; incomplete polls remain in the raw record. Native budget observations and all first/repeat timings are included below. This thin parser profile excludes repository graphs and history concurrency; separate mixed, source-pressure and deeper-history comparisons qualify those. The initial healthy-job characterization preserves the superseded implementation's observations before pending-retirement hardening. PHP AST/semantic consumer budgets, production repository histories and native operating systems remain open. No target application, dependency, runtime, compiler, plugin or hook executes.
